- **SETUP_GIT_HOOKS**: &gt; **Layer:** housekeeping/infra (no layer agent of its own) · **Dispatches:** [`hooks-engineer`](../../agents/hooks-engineer.md) (`sonnet`) · **Outcome:** infra files (hooks + `package.json` prepare wiring, left uncommitted) · **Argument:** `[rebuild]` ([isabelle-barp/agent-brain](https://github.com/isabelle-barp/agent-brain/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/SETUP_GIT_HOOKS.md))
- **SETUP_GITHUB**: &gt; **Layer:** housekeeping/infra · **Dispatches:** [`repository-discovery`](../../agents/repository-discovery.md) (`sonnet`) in the narrow `github-setup` scope · **Outcome:** wiring — installs `.github/` payload + syncs remote labels, single infra commit · **Argument:** none ([isabelle-barp/agent-brain](https://github.com/isabelle-barp/agent-brain/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/SETUP_GITHUB.md))

- **shadcn-init**: Scaffolds the shadcn/ui setup in a React project — detects the framework, walks through `npx shadcn@latest init`, writes or checks components.json, the cn util (@/lib/utils), the Tailwind v4 theme tokens (globals.css) and optionally a dark-mode provider. ([zone1987/claude-a-dev-team](https://github.com/zone1987/claude-a-dev-team/blob/HEAD/plugins/shadcn/commands/shadcn-init.md) · ★1)
- **shadcn-migrate**: Migrate a component from sms-core-ui-2 to shadcn/ui ([lgsf/rl-admin](https://github.com/lgsf/rl-admin/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/shadcn-migrate.md))
- **shadcn-motion**: Invoke the shadcn-motion skill with an explicit mode for course pages, dashboards, hero sections, and interactive experiences ([manutej/ceti-lookbook](https://github.com/manutej/ceti-lookbook/blob/HEAD/plugin/commands/shadcn-motion.md))
- **shadcn-plan**: Prepare the whole shadcn -&gt; Phlex port: upgrade the React catalog to latest shadcn, extract the oracle, generate themes, build the wave plan and run the per-component planners (spec + scenarios). Idempotent. ([doncan-orozco/chadcn](https://github.com/doncan-orozco/chadcn/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/shadcn-plan.md))
- **shadcn-registry**: Scaffolds a shadcn-compatible registry of your own — writes registry.json and registry-item.json to schema, the shadcn build step, hosting and consumer notes (components.json registries) and optionally MCP compatibility. ([zone1987/claude-a-dev-team](https://github.com/zone1987/claude-a-dev-team/blob/HEAD/plugins/shadcn/commands/shadcn-registry.md) · ★1)
- **shadcn-run**: Orchestrate the full shadcn -&gt; Phlex port end to end: infra wave, pilot checkpoint, then fan-out waves of plan -&gt; build -&gt; gate -&gt; triage -&gt; fix, resumable. One call, no human input. ([doncan-orozco/chadcn](https://github.com/doncan-orozco/chadcn/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/shadcn-run.md))
- **shadcn-theme**: Creates or changes a shadcn/ui theme — sets the semantic CSS variable tokens consistently for light and dark, maps them through Tailwind v4 @theme (oklch), adjusts --radius and the --chart tokens, and checks contrast. ([zone1987/claude-a-dev-team](https://github.com/zone1987/claude-a-dev-team/blob/HEAD/plugins/shadcn/commands/shadcn-theme.md) · ★1)
- **shadcn-to-figma**: Generate Figma architecture blueprint from shadcn component code ([neha-tanti/Neha-Notes](https://github.com/neha-tanti/Neha-Notes/blob/HEAD/.opencode/commands/shadcn-to-figma.md))
- **shadcn-to-figma**: Generate a Figma architecture blueprint from shadcn component code and usage patterns using the Designer agent. ([neha-tanti/Neha-Notes](https://github.com/neha-tanti/Neha-Notes/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/shadcn-to-figma.md))
- **shadcn-ui**: Install and configure shadcn/ui components ([jezweb/claude-skills](https://github.com/jezweb/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/frontend/commands/shadcn-ui.md) · ★1.1k · 2 copies)

- **shader**: A `&lt;shader&gt;` item is a GLSL ES 3.00 fragment shader. For its time range it reads the composite of every track beneath it and draws the frame that replaces it. Tracks above it draw over the result as usual, and several shaders stack in track order. Where nothing is beneath it, the composite is transparent black, so a shader can draw a whole frame on its own. ([MdSadiqMd/dotfiles](https://github.com/MdSadiqMd/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/agents/skills/cardboard/commands/shader.md))

- **shannon-gap**: Compute the link's **Strehl ratio** — its achieved spectral efficiency divided by the theoretical Shannon ceiling for the same SNR and bandwidth. In optics, Strehl is the achieved peak intensity over the diffraction-limited ideal; here it is achieved bits/Hz over the information-theoretic ideal. A perfect modem sits near 1.0; every dB you bleed to impairments drops it. ([DaxxSec/cognitropy-lab](https://github.com/DaxxSec/cognitropy-lab/blob/HEAD/satellite-communication-protocols/.claude/commands/shannon-gap.md) · ★1)

- **shard-init**: Scaffold a conductor workspace - contract/, shards/, manifest, and choose the surface adapter(s). ([robworks-code/sharding](https://github.com/robworks-code/sharding/blob/HEAD/commands/shard-init.md))
- **shard-new**: Register a new shard - directory, charter, sandbox guardrails, and manifest entry. ([robworks-code/sharding](https://github.com/robworks-code/sharding/blob/HEAD/commands/shard-new.md))
- **shard-orchestrate**: Drive a session per shard wave by wave, then run the phase gate. Conductor-only. ([robworks-code/sharding](https://github.com/robworks-code/sharding/blob/HEAD/commands/shard-orchestrate.md))
- **shard-phase-check**: Gate the current phase - all participating shards must be clean, acknowledged, and the acceptance suite must pass. ([robworks-code/sharding](https://github.com/robworks-code/sharding/blob/HEAD/commands/shard-phase-check.md))
- **shard-plan**: Show the current phase's shards ordered into parallel waves, with any unprovided slices. ([robworks-code/sharding](https://github.com/robworks-code/sharding/blob/HEAD/commands/shard-plan.md))
- **shard-status**: Show the shard graph, current phase, contract version, and computed blast radius. ([robworks-code/sharding](https://github.com/robworks-code/sharding/blob/HEAD/commands/shard-status.md))
